* I must ensure the workspace root is used for relative path display in the recent files list.
* I will follow existing codebase patterns: EditorConfig for persistence, ModalId/ModalState for UI, and the Cmd system for side effects.

Load skills: brainstorming

Implement the Recent Files feature (all 5 phases) for the Token editor. The feature design is at docs/feature/recent-files.md.

Key implementation plan:
1. Create `src/recent_files.rs` with `RecentEntry` and `RecentFiles` structs (serde JSON serialization, max 50 entries, MRU ordering, add/remove/clear/prune_missing methods, load/save to `~/.config/token-editor/recent.json`)
2. Add `recent_files_path()` to `src/config_paths.rs`
3. Add `recent_files: RecentFiles` field to `AppModel` in `src/model/mod.rs`, load in `AppModel::new()`
4. Hook file opens: in `src/update/app.rs` where `AppMsg::FileLoaded` and `AppMsg::OpenFileDialogResult` are handled, call `model.recent_files.add()` and save
5. Also hook in `create_initial_session()` for CLI file opens
6. Add `ModalId::RecentFiles` variant to `src/model/ui.rs`, create `RecentFilesState` struct with filtered entries, navigation
7. Add keybinding `Cmd+E` → open recent files modal in `keymap.yaml`
8. Add `CommandId::RecentFiles` to `src/commands.rs` with command palette entry
9. Handle the modal in `src/update/ui.rs` (or modal handler) for navigation/selection/open
10. Render the modal in `src/view/mod.rs` following existing modal rendering patterns (command palette style)

Conventions: Follow existing code patterns (EditorConfig for persistence, ModalId/ModalState for modals, Cmd system for side effects). Use `serde_json` for recent.json (already in Cargo.toml deps? check first, may need to add). The workspace root should be passed to RecentEntry for relative path display.

Run `make test && make lint` to verify after implementation.

Rendering utilities exposed for benchmarks\n33: \n34:     /// Blend a foreground color onto a background color with a separate alpha value.\n35:     ///\n36:     /// This is the integer-based blend used for glyph rendering where the alpha\n37:     /// comes from the glyph bitmap coverage value.\n38:     #[inline]\n39:     pub fn blend_pixel_u8(bg: u32, fg: u32, alpha: u8) -> u32 {\n40:         let a = alpha as u32;\n41:         let inv_a = 255 - a;\n42: \n43:         let bg_r = (bg >> 16) & 0xFF;\n44:         let bg_g = (bg >> 8) & 0xFF;\n45:         let bg_b = bg & 0xFF;\n46: \n47:         let fg_r = (fg >> 16) & 0xFF;\n48:         let fg_g = (fg >> 8) & 0xFF;\n49:         let fg_b = fg & 0xFF;\n50: \n51:         let r = (fg_r * a + bg_r * inv_a) / 255;\n52:         let g = (fg_g * a + bg_g * inv_a) / 255;\n53:         let b = (fg_b * a + bg_b * inv_a) / 255;\n54: \n55:         0xFF000000 | (r << 16) | (g << 8) | b\n56:     }\n57: }\n58: \n59: // Re-export commonly used types\n60: pub use commands::Cmd;\n61: pub use config::EditorConfig;\n62: pub use messages::Msg;\n63: pub use model::AppModel;\n64: pub use theme::Theme;",

to keep\nconst MAX_ENTRIES: usize = 50;\n\n/// A single entry in the recent files list\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct RecentEntry {\n    /// Absolute path to the file\n    pub path: PathBuf,\n    /// Timestamp when last opened (Unix epoch seconds)\n    pub opened_at: u64,\n    /// Workspace root when file was opened (if any)\n    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n    pub workspace: Option<PathBuf>,\n    /// Number of times file has been opened (for ranking)\n    #[serde(default)]\n    pub open_count: u32,\n}\n\nimpl RecentEntry {\n    /// Create a new entry for the current time\n    pub fn new(path: PathBuf, workspace: Option<PathBuf>) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            path,\n            opened_at: now_epoch_secs(),\n            workspace,\n            open_count: 1,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Update entry for re-opening\n    pub fn touch(&mut self) {\n        self.opened_at = now_epoch_secs();\n        self.open_count += 1;\n    }\n\n    /// Get display path (relative to workspace if available, otherwise filename)\n    pub fn display_path(&self) -> String {\n        if let Some(ws) = &self.workspace {\n            if let Ok(relative) = self.path.strip_prefix(ws) {\n                return relative.to_string_lossy().to_string();\n            }\n        }\n        self.path\n            .file_name()\n            .map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().to_string())\n            .unwrap_or_else(|| self.path.to_string_lossy().to_string())\n    }\n\n    /// Get human-readable time since opened\n    pub fn time_ago(&self) -> String {\n        let now = now_epoch_secs();\n        let diff = now.saturating_sub(self.opened_at);\n\n        if diff < 60 {\n            \"just now\".to_string()\n        } else if diff < 3600 {\n            let mins = diff / 60;\n            format!(\"{} min{} ago\", mins, if mins == 1 { \"\" } else { \"s\" })\n        } else if diff < 86400 {\n            let hours = diff / 3600;\n            format!(\"{} hour{} ago\", hours, if hours == 1 { \"\" } else { \"s\" })\n        } else if diff < 604800 {\n            let days = diff / 86400;\n            format!(\"{} day{} ago\", days, if days == 1 { \"\" } else { \"s\" })\n        } else {\n            let weeks = diff / 604800;\n            format!(\"{} week{} ago\", weeks, if weeks == 1 { \"\" } else { \"s\" })\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Check if file still exists\n    pub fn exists(&self) -> bool {\n        self.path.exists()\n    }\n}\n\nfn now_epoch_secs() -> u64 {\n    SystemTime::now()\n        .duration_since(SystemTime::UNIX_EPOCH)\n        .map(|d| d.as_secs())\n        .unwrap_or(0)\n}\n\n/// Persistent recent files list\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct RecentFiles {\n    /// Schema version for forward compatibility\n    #[serde(default)]\n    pub version: u32,\n    /// Recent file entries, most recent first\n    pub entries: Vec<RecentEntry>,\n}\n\nimpl RecentFiles {\n    pub const CURRENT_VERSION: u32 = 1;\n\n    /// Load recent files from disk\n    pub fn load() -> Self {\n        let Some(path) = crate::config_paths::recent_files_path() else {\n            return Self::default();\n        };\n        match std::fs::read_to_string(&path) {\n            Ok(contents) => {\n                let mut recent: Self = serde_json::from_str(&contents).unwrap_or_default();\n                recent.prune_missing();\n                recent\n            }\n            Err(_) => Self::default(),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Save recent files to disk\n    pub fn save(&self) -> std::io::Result<()> {\n        let Some(path) = crate::config_paths::recent_files_path() else {\n            return Err(std::io::Error::new(\n                std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound,\n                \"No config directory available\",\n            ));\n        };\n        crate::config_paths::ensure_all_config_dirs();\n        let contents = serde_json::to_string_pretty(self)?;\n        std::fs::write(path, contents)\n    }\n\n    /// Add a file to recent list (or update if already present)\n    pub fn add(&mut self, path: PathBuf, workspace: Option<PathBuf>) {\n        // Canonicalize path for consistent matching\n        let canonical = {\n        self.entries.clear();\n    }\n\n    /// Prune entries for files that no longer exist\n    pub fn prune_missing(&mut self) {\n        let original_len = self.entries.len();\n The pattern to follow:\n- For text input operations (InsertChar, SetInput, DeleteBackward, DeleteWordBackward, DeleteForward, Paste): use `state.editable.xxx()` and reset `state.selected_index = 0` when input changes\n- For cursor movement (MoveCursorLeft, MoveCursorRight, MoveCursorHome, MoveCursorEnd, MoveCursorWordLeft, MoveCursorWordRight and their WithSelection variants): use `state.editable.xxx()`\n- For SelectAll: use `state.editable.select_all()`\n- For Copy: use `state.editable.selected_text()`\n- For Cut: use `state.editable.cut()` and reset selected_index\n- For SelectPrevious: `state.selected_index = state.selected_index.saturating_sub(1);` return None for preview_theme_id\n- For SelectNext: \n```\nlet filtered = state.filtered_entries();\nlet max_index = filtered.len().saturating_sub(1);\nstate.selected_index = state.selected_index.saturating_add(1).min(max_index);\n```\nreturn None for preview_theme_id\n- For Confirm: \n```\nModalState::RecentFiles(state) => {\n    let filtered = state.filtered_entries();\n    if let Some(entry) = filtered.get(state.selected_index) {\n        let path = entry.path.clone();\n        model.ui.close_modal();\n        return update_layout(model, LayoutMsg::OpenFileInNewTab(path));\n    }\n    model.ui.close_modal();\n    Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n}\n```\n\nSearch for every `ModalState::ThemePicker` match arm in the file and add the corresponding `ModalState::RecentFiles(state)` arm after it (or after the FileFinder arm if that's more natural).\n\nBe careful not to miss any match blocks. There are approximately 20+ match arms that need updating.\n\nAlso, the handlers for:\n- ModalMsg::SetInput: `state.editable.set_content(&text); state.selected_index = 0;`\n- ModalMsg::DeleteForward: `state.editable.delete_forward(); state.selected_index = 0;`\n\nWhen done, verify the file compiles by running: `cargo check 2>&1 | head -30`\n",
  "description": "Add RecentFiles arms to all modal message handlers in update/ui.rs"
}

## Assistant

Here's a summary of the Recent Files feature implementation across all 5 phases:

**Phase 1 - Core Data Structures:**
- Created `src/recent_files.rs` with `RecentEntry` and `RecentFiles` structs (serde JSON, MRU ordering, max 50 entries, add/remove/clear/prune_missing)
- Added `recent_files_path()` to `src/config_paths.rs`
- 9 unit tests passing

**Phase 2 - AppModel Integration:**
- Added `recent_files: RecentFiles` field to `AppModel`
- Loads from `~/.config/token-editor/recent.json` on startup
- Added `record_file_opened()` method with async save (background thread)

**Phase 3 - File Open Hooks:**
- `AppMsg::FileLoaded` in `src/update/app.rs` — records loaded files
- `open_file_in_new_tab()` in `src/update/layout.rs` — records tab opens
- CLI file args in `src/runtime/app.rs` — records initial files

**Phase 4 - Recent Files Modal:**
- Added `ModalId::RecentFiles` and `RecentFilesState` with filter input
- Added `CommandId::OpenRecentFiles` with command palette entry
- Added `Command::OpenRecentFiles` to keymap system with `Cmd+E` binding
- Full modal message handling (navigation, filter, selection, confirm → opens file)

**Phase 5 - Rendering:**
- Modal renders with title, filter input, scrollable file list with file icons, display paths, and right-aligned "time ago" text
- Reuses `file_finder_layout` geometry for consistent sizing
- Hit-testing support for click handling

## Assistant

Both fixes applied:

1. **Current file excluded** — `RecentFilesState::new()` now takes the current file path and filters it out, so the top entry is always the *previous* file. Cmd+E → Enter swaps to it, then Cmd+E → Enter swaps back.

2. **Icon removed** — The Nerd Font glyphs can't render with the editor's built-in font, so the recent files list now shows just the display path without the broken icon character.

So:\n  \n    + Only patch `Regular` font and remove any other fonts.\n    + Only patch `JetBrainsMono-Regular.ttf` and output `JetBrainsMonoNerdFont-Regular.ttf` .\n\n# License\n\n* Codes: [MIT License](https://github.com/ryanoasis/nerd-fonts/blob/master/LICENSE)\n* Fonts: [OFL-1.1 License](https://github.com/JetBrains/JetBrainsMono/blob/master/OFL.txt)\n\n# Usage\n\n**NOTICE** : This repository can update automatically by `Github Actions` . (As long as you can install the following dependencies correctly.)\n    + `Winget` , i.e., [winget-cli](https://github.com/microsoft/winget-cli)\n    + Other Tools:\n        \n        - [PSComputerManagementZp](https://www.powershellgallery.com/packages/PSComputerManagementZp)\n        - [FontForge](https://fontforge.org/en-US/downloads/)\n        - [gftools builder](https://googlefonts.github.io/gf-guide/build.html)\n        - [fonttools](https://fonttools.readthedocs.io/en/latest/)\n* Steps:\n  \n  ```\n  git clone https: // github.com / Zhaopudark / JetBrainsMonoNerdFonts.git\n  cd JetBrainsMonoNerdFonts\n  \n   Install-Module - Name PSComputerManagementZp - Force\n   Import-Module PSComputerManagementZp\n  \n  winget install -- id FontForge.FontForge -- force #  UAC prompt may occur \n   Add-PathToCurrentProcessEnvPath - Path \" C:\\Program Files (x86)\\FontForgeBuilds\\bin \" \n  \n  pip install - r \" ./requirements.txt \" - q\n   . \" .\\build.ps1 \" $GithubPAT #  consider to provide a Github Personal Access Token\n  ```\n* Get the output font file from `.\\Output\\JetBrainsMonoNerdFont-Regular.ttf` .\n\n### Build on Linux\n\n* Requirements:\n  \n    + `Linux` platform.\n    + [PowerShell 7.x](https://github.com/PowerShell/PowerShell) .\n        \n        - For stable build, you need to provide a `Github Personal Access Token` as a shell variable `$GithubPAT` .\n    + `Python 3.11` or higher. all font files in the folder, right-click any of them, then pick “Install” from the menu.\nUnpack fonts to ~/.local/share/fonts (or /usr/share/fonts, to install fonts system-wide) and fc-cache -f
